===== codex-docs/FIX-154_TECHNICAL_DESIGN.md =====
# FIX-154 ƒ?" Trusted higher-timeframe BUY validation

Status: implementation design approved. FIX-154 is scheduled before the
remaining FIX-153 runtime release work.

## Purpose and boundary

FIX-154 replaces the current binary fresh-1h bearish veto with a trusted
higher-timeframe assessment. It does not relax the existing 5m confirmation
requirement, risk/reward result, current-1m BTC protection, or profile rules
except where a raw 1h decision would otherwise act as an untrusted veto.

The legacy `src/main/resources/md/FIX-146.md` is an unrelated shared-market
readiness design and is not an implementation source for this fix.

## Current integration points to replace

The 1h/5m blocks in all three paths below must be replaced as a whole by one
shared assessment call. Do not add this decision to `FinalDecisionService`.

1. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuy()` ƒ?" direct 1m BUYs.
2. `TradeExecutionValidationService.validateBuyContext()` ƒ?" accumulated/setup
   authority, whose reference may be a 5m signal.
3. `OpportunityConsolidationService.evaluate()` ƒ?" consolidated BUYs.

Each replacement owns the current lookup, freshness, 5m veto, and 1h veto
block in that method. The remaining profile/consolidation logic consumes the
assessment's effective context; it must not reread the raw 1h decision as an
independent veto.

## Immutable signal evidence

Migration `V107` adds:

```sql
ALTER TABLE trade_signal ADD COLUMN data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL;
```

`AnalysisService` writes `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` through the
existing `TradeSignal.builder()` call for every newly generated signal on every
interval. Historic rows are never backfilled: `NULL` means unknown/invalid.

FIX-154 does not depend on `signal_mode` or any other FIX-153 runtime/schema
feature. `data_quality_valid = true` is sufficient evidence eligibility for
this release; false or `NULL` is unusable. Historical-only exclusion is added
later with FIX-153, after its own migration/runtime release is approved.

Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` is audit evidence only. It is never a
FIX-154 usability or hard-veto predicate. The current 1m BTC guard is unchanged.

## Time-bounded selection and fixed freshness

For a request with reference signal `R`, use `R.generatedAt` as `evaluatedAt`.
Select the latest same-symbol 5m and 1h signal such that both its generated
time and its candle close are at or before `evaluatedAt`; no wall-clock lookup
is permitted. Replay supplies the same historical reference time.

Existing fixed freshness limits remain authoritative:

| Interval | Maximum age |
|---|---:|
| 5m | 20 minutes |
| 1h | 3 hours |

`MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` and `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` retain their existing
meaning and priority when their respective interval is missing or stale. A
stale signal is not quietly converted into an allowed BUY just because it is
`NOT_TRUSTED` for FIX-154.

## Usability and 1h strength

A fresh row is usable for FIX-154 only if `data_quality_valid = true`.

FIX-154 v1 does not invent ADX/DMI/VWAP/CMF evidence, because those indicators
are not currently persisted. Its fixed strength definition is:

```text
HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE = 1h decision == STRONG_SELL
                         AND confidence_score >= 70
SOFT_CONFLICT          = fresh, quality-valid 1h decision == SELL
                         OR STRONG_SELL with confidence_score below 70
NOT_TRUSTED            = fresh but quality-invalid/unknown/historical-only 1h
                         OR fresh non-bearish 1h
```

`confidence_score` is the persisted effective confidence. `raw_confidence_score`
is explicitly not used. The constant `70` is a named FIX-154 configuration
default, persisted in the audit row, and may change only through a reviewed
strategy change with Replay regression.

## Exact 1h A- 5m decision matrix

First apply existing missing/stale checks. Only then apply this table.

| 1h state | 5m state | Final HTF result before profile | Profile inputs |
|---|---|---|---|
| missing/stale | any | existing `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` rejection | none |
| any | missing/stale | existing `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` rejection | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | `1H_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| NOT_TRUSTED non-bearish from false/NULL quality | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |
| SOFT_CONFLICT | bearish | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| SOFT_CONFLICT | bullish/neutral/watch | continue to profile, cap resulting first entry at 25% | effective 1h=`NEUTRAL`, raw 5m |
| HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bearish, quality-valid | legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO`; audit `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` | none |
| HARD_BEARISH_CANDIDATE | bullish/neutral/watch, quality-valid | `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO` | none |
| fresh, quality-valid non-bearish 1h | non-bearish | continue to profile | raw 1h, raw 5m |

The profile call produces the final decision for every `continue to profile`
row. It may still return its existing insufficient-confirmation code. It must
not consult the raw 1h row after this mapping.

`5m quality=false/NULL` does not alter the legacy 5m fallback. A fresh raw
bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO`, exactly as today. It merely cannot
act as new FIX-154 hard-veto confirmation or a soft-conflict release.

The veto order is fixed: a bearish 5m returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any
trusted-1h result. If the 1h is hard, audit records `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT`
on that legacy outcome. When 5m is not bearish, a hard trusted 1h returns
`HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`. The only deliberate lasting relaxation is a trusted
SOFT bearish 1h: an otherwise allowed initial entry is capped at 25%.

## Profile contract

`conservativeBuy`, `balancedBuy`, `balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException`, and
`aggressiveBuy` receive the assessment's effective 1h/5m contextƒ?"not raw
selected 1h evidence. A SOFT conflict maps to `NEUTRAL`, never `WATCH`, then
the validation result is capped with `min(profilePositionPercent, 25)`.
This means the neutral-5m/direct-BUY exception still requires a genuinely
bullish 1h and cannot be unlocked by SOFT evidence. An untrusted non-bearish
1h retains its raw decision so deployment does not accidentally tighten current
bullish authority.

The assessment result carries `maxInitialPercent`: `25` for `SOFT_CONFLICT`,
otherwise no cap. Every caller that selects a size must use
`min(itsCalculatedPercent, maxInitialPercent)` when the cap is present. This
includes direct validation, `validateBuyContext()` callers (the 5m-trigger
path and accumulated evidence), and
`OpportunityConsolidationService.consolidatedPositionPercent()`.

The raw selected signals remain audit evidence. Existing 5m requirements inside
profiles remain unchanged. HARD and untrusted-bearish outcomes never call a
profile method.

### Add-on contract

`ExecutionIntelligenceService.progressivePositionDecision()` already reads raw
1h evidence and requires `WATCH` or bullish support for `SCOUT_ENTRY`,
`CONFIRMATION_ADD`, and `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`. Keep that check unchanged and
outside the three replacement paths; it already blocks HARD, SOFT, and
untrusted-bearish 1h states. Do not route it through effective FIX-154 context,
which could widen behavior. When that existing check rejects while the assessed
band is SOFT, record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` as audit/metric evidence only.

## Append-only execution audit

FIX-154 is evaluated during execution/consolidation, not while a signal is
saved. Migration `V107` creates append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`:

- primary key ID;
- reference signal ID, evaluation path (`DIRECT_BUY`, `BUY_CONTEXT`,
  `CONSOLIDATION`, `PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD`, `CONFIRMATION_ADD`,
  `ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_ADD`) and `evaluated_at`; no unique constraint;
- raw/effective 1h and 5m signal IDs, decisions, signal modes, candle closes,
  ages, data-quality values, and usability reasons;
- configured confidence threshold, band, legacy-veto result, profile context,
  final result/reason, Production/Replay mode, and creation timestamp.

The audit is written best-effort after the relevant execution/consolidation
decision is known. A duplicate or persistence failure is logged and increments
`fix154.audit_write_failure_count`; it must never roll back, reject, or alter a
trading decision. It is evidence onlyƒ?"not a fence or an input to later trading.

## Deployment behavior

Immediately after deployment all pre-V107 1h/5m rows have
`data_quality_valid=NULL`. They are treated as `NOT_TRUSTED`; they cannot make a
new permissive decision: a bearish unknown 1h remains blocked, while a
non-bearish unknown 1h retains its existing profile authority. The health view
must expose `fix154.untrusted_bearish_1h_block_count` and
`fix154.audit_write_failure_count`, `fix154.soft_capped_entry_count`, and
`fix154.soft_add_blocked_count`, grouped by execution path.

## Migration ordering and FIX-153 independence

The configured local database has V106 as its latest applied Flyway migration.
Therefore FIX-154 reserves `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`.
Before this migration is applied anywhere, rename the currently unreleased
`V107__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql` to V108. Never rename or edit
an applied migration. Do not enable Flyway `outOfOrder`; deployment order is
made explicit instead.

## Required regression suite

- All three integration paths use the shared service and no longer contain raw
  `1H_BEARISH_VETO` branches.
- `STRONG_SELL` + `confidence_score >= 70` and bearish fresh valid 5m produces
  legacy `5M_BEARISH_VETO` with `HARD_CONFIRMATION_PRESENT` audit; the same 1h
  with non-bearish 5m produces `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
- Strong/soft/untrusted 1h plus bullish, neutral, watch, bearish, missing,
  stale, false-quality, and NULL-quality 5m covers every matrix row and its
  final profile result.
- `MISSING_1H_CONTEXT` (3h) and `MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION` (20m) retain their
  priority and codes.
- BNB #150a: persisted historical 1h bearish row with `NULL` quality =>
  `NOT_TRUSTED` and `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
- BNB #150b: Replay regenerates that same 1h with quality evidence and it is
  SOFT; no trusted hard veto is emitted and the final expected result is
  `REJECTED_RISK_REWARD` when the risk/reward guard fails.
- Historical `btcContextEntryAllowed=false` does not change usability; current
  1m BTC behavior is unchanged.
- FIX-154 migration succeeds with no `signal_mode` column and its assessment
  has no FIX-153 schema/runtime dependency.
- No future signal/candle is selected in Production or Replay; both produce
  equivalent audit evidence for the same historical inputs.
- Pre-migration `NULL` quality is never treated as valid.
- Repeated execution/consolidation evaluations append audit rows and audit
  failure leaves the business decision intact.
- A SOFT initial entry never exceeds 25% through progressive, confirmation, or
  accumulated-evidence or consolidation sizing; existing progressive raw-1h
  support rules block add stages while the 1h remains bearish.

## Release conditions

Run Java 21 unit/integration tests, migration tests, Production/Replay parity,
and a bounded no-look-ahead historical replay before enabling FIX-154.
FIX-153 remains independent and retains its own release gates.

## Revision addendum ƒ?" Round 5 approval conditions

This addendum governs where it differs from earlier wording.

### Release build decision

The FIX-154 release build includes `V108__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql`
after the current unreleased FIX-153 V107 migration is renumbered to V108.
This is required even with reconciliation disabled: `candle_analysis_outcome`
is used by ordinary live signal processing, terminal worker outcomes, and the
closed-candle detector. Omitting V108 would cause missing-table SQL failures;
in particular, a normal signal-processing completion would roll back because
its outcome write is inside the processing transaction.

Including V108 ships FIX-153 reconciliation *schema only*. The reconciliation
runtime remains disabled and retains all of its separate release gates.

### No FIX-153 provenance dependency

Ignore earlier references to historical-only status or signal modes in the
FIX-154 band definition and audit payload. FIX-154 v1 eligibility depends only
on time-bounded selection and `data_quality_valid = true`; `signal_mode` is not
read, required, or stored by the FIX-154 audit.

### Effective context is transient

The assessment returns immutable effective decision values separately from the
loaded `TradeSignal` entities. No code may call `setDecision`, mutate, merge,
or save a selected 1h/5m signal to obtain effective `NEUTRAL` context. Profile
methods receive explicit effective decisions (or an immutable assessment
context), while the raw entity remains audit evidence only. A regression test
must load a SOFT 1h signal, execute the assessment/profile path, flush/clear,
and prove its stored decision is unchanged.

### Progressive-path evidence is observational

`progressivePositionDecision()` retains its existing raw-1h support check as
the only decision authority for scout/add paths. If that existing check passes,
FIX-154 performs no assessment lookup and writes no progressive audit row. If
it rejects because raw 1h is bearish, a best-effort audit-only assessment may
record `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` when the band is SOFT. That observation must not
alter the rejection, allow an add, cause a database lookup on passing paths, or
propagate an audit failure into the trading decision.

V108 ships with FIX-154 because the current runtime writes
`candle_analysis_outcome`. Reconciliation remains disabled.

### Round 5 text clarification

This addendum removes any earlier contrary implication: FIX-154 v1 has no
`signal_mode` or historical-only predicate or audit field. Effective 1h/5m
decisions are separate immutable values and never mutate a loaded signal.
Progressive-path assessment is audit-only after its existing raw-1h check has
already rejected; it is skipped entirely when that check passes and never feeds
the scout/add decision.

## Implementation completion record ƒ?" 2026-10-10

The approved FIX-154 design is now implemented in the working tree.

- `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` creates the nullable signal
  quality evidence and append-only assessment audit table. No historical value
  is fabricated or backfilled.
- `AnalysisService` persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` for every new
  signal.
- `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService` owns the hard/soft/untrusted decision
  matrix. It returns immutable effective decisions and does not mutate a JPA
  signal.
- Direct BUY validation, accumulated-evidence authority, and opportunity
  consolidation invoke that one service. A SOFT result uses effective 1h
  `NEUTRAL` and applies the 25% initial-position cap. The legacy bearish-5m
  veto retains precedence. Progressive adds retain their existing raw 1h
  requirement and can only create best-effort audit evidence after rejection.
- `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore` is append-only and runs in an independent
  transaction. It catches insert failures so audit availability cannot alter a
  business decision.

Focused Java 21 verification passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

This is implementation completion, not production deployment approval. Apply
V107/V108 only through the normal clean Java 21 release pipeline and complete
the required migration, Replay-parity, and broader regression validation before
enabling it in Production.


===== codex-docs/FIX-154_IMPLEMENTATION_HANDOFF.md =====
# FIX-154 ƒ?" Implementation Handoff for Code Review

Date: 2026-10-10  
Status: implemented in the local working tree; not deployed.

## Scope

FIX-154 changes only higher-timeframe BUY authority. It does not enable or
change FIX-153 reconciliation/runtime behavior.

The objective is to distinguish trusted, strong bearish 1h evidence from
unknown or weaker evidence without changing a stored historical signal.

## Implemented policy

1. The current 1m BUY still requires fresh 5m and 1h context.
2. A bearish 5m returns the existing `5M_BEARISH_VETO` first.
3. A bearish 1h with `data_quality_valid` `NULL` or `false` returns
   `1H_BEARISH_VETO`.
4. A trusted 1h `STRONG_SELL` with `confidence_score >= 70` returns
   `HTF_TRUSTED_BEARISH_VETO`.
5. A trusted 1h `SELL`, or `STRONG_SELL` with confidence below 70, is a
   SOFT conflict. It passes effective 1h `NEUTRAL` to profile logic and caps
   every allowed initial entry at 25%.
6. A non-bearish 1h with unknown quality keeps its raw decision; FIX-154 does
   not tighten bullish/non-bearish authority merely because historical quality
   is absent.
7. Existing progressive/add logic retains its raw 1h requirement. A SOFT
   conflict cannot authorize an add. Optional `SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED` evidence
   is observational only.

## Database changes

`V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql` adds:

- `trade_signal.data_quality_valid BOOLEAN NULL`.
- Replay parity columns on `trade_signal_test`:
  `btc_context_candle_close_time`, `btc_context_age_seconds`,
  `btc_context_freshness_threshold_seconds`, `signal_mode`, and
  `data_quality_valid`.
- Append-only `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, including effective/raw decisions,
  candle-close-based age, quality evidence, the hard-confirmation marker,
  cap, legacy veto and final result.

Historical `data_quality_valid` stays `NULL`; no value is backfilled.

## Included production code

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `domain/TradeSignal.java` | Maps nullable `data_quality_valid`. |
| `service/AnalysisService.java` | Persists `MarketContextSnapshot.dataValid()` at signal generation. |
| `service/HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.java` | Pure FIX-154 decision matrix; returns detached effective decisions and never mutates entities. |
| `service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java` | Applies assessment to direct BUY and contextual/accumulated authority; enforces SOFT cap; records non-blocking audit evidence. |
| `service/OpportunityConsolidationService.java` | Applies the same matrix and cap to consolidation. |
| `execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java` | Keeps existing progressive raw-1h guard; invokes optional audit-only SOFT evidence after its rejection. Uses `SETUP_WAKEUP` audit path for the 5m wake-up caller. |
| `service/BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore.java` | Snapshots and asynchronously persists best-effort Production audit rows. Replay writes no Production audit row. |
| `config/Fix154AuditAsyncConfig.java` | Dedicated one-thread, bounded executor; audit saturation drops evidence rather than borrowing a trading connection. |
| `service/Fix154Metrics.java` | Non-gating counters for untrusted 1h blocks, audit failures, SOFT caps and SOFT add blocks. |
| `service/Fix154DiagnosticsController.java` | Read-only `GET /api/fix154/health` metrics endpoint. |
| `service/Fix154RuntimeSchemaChecks.java` | Checks V107-required production and Replay schema before runtime admission. |

## Replay behavior

The regular reflection-based Replay writer persists every JPA `TradeSignal`
column. V107 therefore updates `trade_signal_test` alongside production.
FIX-154 audit rows are deliberately skipped while `ExecutionReplayScope` is
active because replay-generated entities do not have Production IDs and parity
is recorded by Replayƒ?Ts own evidence tables.

## Tests included

- `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`
  - hard, soft, untrusted, 5m-veto precedence, 69/70 boundary, BNB #150a and
    #150b policy cases, and unknown non-bearish authority.
- `TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`
  - direct/context SOFT caps, conservative behavior, neutral-5m exception is
    not unlocked, audit failure isolation, Production/Replay parity, and
    future-context exclusion.
- `OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`
  - SOFT cap through consolidation.
- `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStoreTest`
  - Replay does not write a Production audit row.
- `Fix154MigrationContractTest`
  - V107 contains production, Replay and audit schema contracts.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Frozen population remains exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. The permitted `db/CODEX` directory has no result artifacts; no trade-specific DB fact is available.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current entry route is:

  `trade_signal` ƒ+' `signal_processing_work` claim/freshness check ƒ+' execution validation ƒ+' canonical fresh price (ƒ%15 seconds) ƒ+' wallet BUY ƒ+' `paper_position` OPEN.

  A completed processing-work record does not prove entry; the durable `paper_position` and corresponding executed wallet BUY are required. [SignalProcessingCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:48), [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340), [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:30).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** For a new BUY, wallet execution occurs before the `paper_position` is created. The position uses the canonical execution price but timestamps itself with a separate `Instant.now()`. Therefore, entry timing must be reconciled against `wallet_trade.executed_at`; `paper_position.opened_at` alone is not necessarily the exact wallet execution instant. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:375).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current mechanical-exit priority is: take-profit checkpoint/extension, stop loss, profit-lock hard floor, profit-lock policy exit, normal signal-driven exit, then near-TP partial harvest. A loss therefore requires particular scrutiny of stop-loss evidence and any earlier price events; candle OHLC is not a substitute for accepted live price evidence. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:99).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** In the direct-price route, a canonical price event is first persisted as `PENDING`; protection then atomically records `APPLIED` or `NON_CANONICAL`. `APPLIED` proves completion of the protection callback, but may still be a no-op when no open position exists. `PENDING` is unresolved, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` is not exit evidence. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:45), [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:53).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The two closing routes have materially different evidence ordering:
  - Signal-route `completeClose` persists `paper_position` first, then attempts a wallet exit and catches wallet exceptions.
  - Live protection executes the mechanical wallet exit first, then closes `paper_position`.
  
  Thus a paper/wallet mismatch cannot be interpreted without checking both tables and the exit audit. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487), [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:165).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** A current-code audit limitation: `LivePositionProtectionService.closePaper` records `close_reason` and immutable `production_exit_audit`, but does not populate `paper_position.exit_signal_id`. Consequently, a null exit signal cannot prove that a live mechanical close had no signal context. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391).

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** None of the above establishes which route closed any frozen trade. The running/deployed code and configuration at each historical entry/exit are also unproven.

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `paper_position` records entry and exit links, prices, lifecycle times, close reason, and P&L. [PaperPosition.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:10)
- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `production_exit_audit` is intended as immutable terminal-route evidence keyed to `paper_position_id`.
- **EXTERNAL RESEARCH:** None.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The study remains restricted to frozen `crypto_ai.paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitute trades were used.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `agent-docs/runs/fts-WINDOW2-20261107-225520/db/CODEX/` has no readable evidence artifacts. Therefore, no durable position, signal, wallet, price-event, candle, audit, or runtime-log evidence exists in this track to identify the actual root cause of any loss.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** A trade-specific root-cause finding is currently **not established** for any of the six trades. It would be unsound to classify them as bad entries, valid stop losses, delayed exits, execution defects, or data-quality defects without their durable records.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current source has materially different close evidence paths:
  - Mechanical protection executes the wallet exit before closing `paper_position`.
  - Signal-route close persists the `paper_position` closure before attempting the wallet exit, and catches wallet exceptions.
  
  Any position/wallet mismatch is therefore an investigation lead, not automatically proof of an execution failure. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487), [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:165)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** A negative realized P&L could result from a `STOP_LOSS`, a normal signal-driven exit, or another close route. Current protection treats stop loss as absolute when the accepted price is at or below the configured stop. This is not proof that any selected trade was stopped out. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:174)

- **EXTERNAL RESEARCH:** Binance marks a WebSocket kline explicitly with `x` (ƒ?ois this kline closed?ƒ??), while REST klines are uniquely identified by open time and supply OHLC data. Consequently, candle OHLC cannot by itself establish the accepted live-price event or paper-wallet fill that caused a close; those must come from trader-side price-event and wallet evidence. [Binance WebSocket kline documentation](https://developers.binance.com/en/docs/catalog/core-trading-spot-trading/api/ws-streams/~), [Binance REST kline documentation](https://developers.binance.com/en/docs/catalog/core-trading-spot-trading/api/rest-api/market)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The supplied snapshot labels remain internally inconsistent: UTC `2026-11-07 19:55:22` converts to **KSA `2026-11-07 22:55:22`**, not the supplied `22:55:20`. This does not change the frozen IDs; it prevents treating the provided KSA snapshot label as an exact timestamp conversion.

## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current entry rules include final execution-price authority limited to a 15-second-old canonical price observation; the historical deployed version and configuration remain unproven. [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:24)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current source rejects initial BUYs below its 50-point entry-quality cutoff as `CHASE_ENTRY_BLOCKED`; this is a present-code control, not historical proof for these trades. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:1171)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Timing persistence is best-effort and incomplete; absence of timing evidence cannot prove no delay.

===== PaperPosition =====
package com.crypto.domain;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

@Entity
@Table(name = "paper_position")
@Getter @Setter @Builder @NoArgsConstructor @AllArgsConstructor
public class PaperPosition {
    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String symbol;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 10)
    private PositionSide side;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private PositionStatus status;

    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal quantity;

    @Column(name = "entry_price", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal entryPrice;

    @Column(name = "stop_loss", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal stopLoss;

    @Column(name = "take_profit", nullable = false, precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal takeProfit;

    @Column(name = "exit_price", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal exitPrice;

    @Column(name = "realized_pnl", precision = 30, scale = 12)
    private BigDecimal realizedPnl;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "signal_id")
    private TradeSignal signal;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "exit_signal_id")
    private TradeSignal exitSignal;

    @Column(name = "close_reason", length = 40)
    private String closeReason;

    // FIX-144: Full signal/execution explanations and progressive additions exceed 2,000 chars.
    // Preserve audit text without truncation; V100 widens the existing physical columns.
    @Column(name = "entry_reason", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String entryReason;

    // FIX-144: Exit audit explanations use the same expanded storage contract.
    @Column(name = "exit_reason", columnDefinition = "MEDIUMTEXT")
    private String exitReason;

    @Column(name = "opened_at", nullable = false)
    private Instant openedAt;

    @Column(name = "closed_at")
    private Instant closedAt;
}

===== ExecutionPriceAuthorityService =====
package com.crypto.execution.service;

import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * FIX-056 fresh execution-price authority.
 *
 * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
 * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
 * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
 * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
 * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
 */
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
    private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);

    private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
    private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;

    public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
        // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
        // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
        // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
        Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
                "Execution price reference time is required");

        if (replayScope.active()) {
            return replayScope.latestPrice()
                    .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
                    .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
                    .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
                    .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
                    .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
        }

        return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
                .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
                .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
    }

    public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
}

===== Signal coordinator =====
package com.crypto.execution.processing;

import com.crypto.domain.PaperPosition;
import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;
import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
import com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.function.Function;

/** FIX-127: one coordinator for all Production signal callers. No retry of the
 * existing body after it has begun; only the new initial lock is retry-marked. */
@Service
public class SignalProcessingCoordinator {
    // FIX-125 mandatory in Spring; null only in existing constructor-only policy tests.
    @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
    private com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletTransactionCoordination walletCoordination;

    private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(SignalProcessingCoordinator.class);
    private final SignalProcessingStore store;
    private final TradeSignalRepository signals;
    private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positions;
    private final SignalProcessingFreshness freshness;
    private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
    public SignalProcessingCoordinator(SignalProcessingStore store, TradeSignalRepository signals,
            WalletManagedPositionRepository positions, SignalProcessingFreshness freshness, PlatformTransactionManager manager) {
        this.store=store;this.signals=signals;this.positions=positions;this.freshness=freshness;
        transaction=new TransactionTemplate(manager);
        transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
    }
    public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
            Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business) {
        return process(signalId,background,business,null);
    }
    public Optional<PaperPosition> process(long signalId, boolean background,
            Function<TradeSignal,Optional<PaperPosition>> business, Long sourceEventId) {
        if(TransactionSynchronizationManager.isActualTransactionActive())
            throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 caller must be outside the processing transaction");
        if(!background && !store.exists(signalId)) {
            // Automatic callers have already registered atomically with creation.
            // Explicit existing-signal calls register only at the user's request.
            transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->store.register(signals.findById(signalId)
                    .orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal not found: "+signalId)),ProcessingOrigin.EXPLICIT));
        }
        store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
        for(int localAttempt=0;localAttempt<2;localAttempt++) {
            var work=store.claim(signalId,background);
            if(work==null) {
                log.info("[FIX-127][NOT_CLAIMED] signalId={}, background={}; no processing performed",signalId,background);
                return Optional.empty();
            }
            log.info("[FIX-127][ATTEMPT] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candleOpenTime={}, origin={}, attempt={}, background={}",
                    signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.candleOpenTime(),work.origin(),work.attempts(),background);
            java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger completion=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger(-1);
            try {
                Optional<PaperPosition> result=transaction.execute(tx->{
                    org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(
                        new org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization() {
                            @Override public void afterCompletion(int status) { completion.set(status); }
                        });
                    // FIX-140: observe the real transaction completion; a thrown exception alone
                    // does not prove rollback. The private marker is only created here.
                    if(walletCoordination!=null) {
                        try { walletCoordination.symbol(work.symbol()); }
                        catch(RuntimeException failure) {
                            if(sourceEventId!=null && retryableSymbolLock(failure))throw new SymbolLockFailure(failure);
                            throw failure;
                        }
                    }
                    var owned=store.locked(signalId);
                    if(owned==null || !"RUNNING".equals(owned.status()) || !work.owner().equals(owned.owner()))
                        throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 processing ownership changed");
                    // Acquire before PositionManagementService inserts a referencing
                    // advisory or executes POSITION_STOP_LOSS. No rules evaluated yet.
                    try {
                        positions.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(work.symbol(),"OPEN");
                    } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
                        if(InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(ex))throw new InitialLockFailure(ex);
                        throw ex;
                    }
                    store.requireSourceOwner(signalId,sourceEventId);
                    // Read signal/freshness after lock acquisition: lock wait must not
                    // consume the recovery grace before it is checked, or establish an
                    // ordinary repeatable-read snapshot before the position is locked.
                    TradeSignal signal=signals.findById(signalId).orElseThrow(()->new IllegalArgumentException("Signal missing"));
                    if(!work.symbol().equals(signal.getSymbol()) || !work.interval().equals(signal.getInterval())
                            || !work.candleOpenTime().equals(signal.getCandleOpenTime()))
                        throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-127 signal lineage changed");
                    // FIX-153: every claimed attempt, including the first direct
                    // attempt regardless of origin, is checked after normal locks.
                    if(!freshness.eligible(work))throw new Expired();
                    Optional<PaperPosition> outcome=business.apply(signal);
                    store.complete(work,outcome.map(PaperPosition::getId).orElse(null));
                    return outcome;
                });
                log.info("[FIX-127][COMPLETED] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}; processing committed (not necessarily a trade)",
                        signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts());
                return result;
            } catch(Expired ex) {
                // Only exact RUNNING ownership can expire this attempt, after business TX rolled back.
                if(store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,"EXPIRED","FRESHNESS","Exact candle is no longer latest and fresh")!=1)
                    throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 expiry ownership unconfirmed: signal="+signalId);
                log.warn("[FIX-127][EXPIRED] signalId={}, symbol={}, attempt={}",signalId,work.symbol(),work.attempts());
                return Optional.empty();
            } catch(RuntimeException ex) {
                // FIX-140: only this invocation's initial symbol-lock marker plus a
                // confirmed rollback grants retry. Unknown connection/commit failures stay review-only.
                if(ex instanceof SymbolLockFailure && completion.get()==
                        org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_ROLLED_BACK) {
                    String result=work.attempts()<4?"SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY":"LOCK_RETRY_EXHAUSTED";
                    int changed=store.finishSymbolLockFailure(work,result,ex.toString());
                    if(changed!=1)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-140 retry persistence unconfirmed",ex);
                    log.warn("[FIX-140][SAFE_RETRY_RESULT] event={}, signal={}, attempt={}, outcome={}, rollback=CONFIRMED",
                        sourceEventId,signalId,work.attempts(),result);
                    if("SYMBOL_LOCK_RETRY".equals(result))throw new SymbolLockRetryScheduled(signalId);
                    return Optional.empty();
                }
                boolean initial=ex instanceof InitialLockFailure;
                String status=initial && work.attempts()<2 ? "RETRYABLE_FAILURE" : "REVIEW_REQUIRED";
                int recorded=store.finishWithoutBusiness(work,status,initial ? "INITIAL_POSITION_LOCK" : "PROCESSING_OR_COMMIT",ex.toString());
                log.error("[FIX-127][{}] signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, attempt={}, initialLock={}",
                        recorded==1 ? status : "FAILURE_RECORD_NOT_UPDATED",signalId,work.symbol(),work.interval(),work.attempts(),initial,ex);
                if(!initial || work.attempts()>=2)throw ex;
                // TransactionTemplate completed rollback before entering this catch.
            }
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }
    // FIX-140: classify only known lock cancellation/deadlock errors. Communications
    // errors are deliberately excluded even when nested below a data-access exception.
    static boolean retryableSymbolLock(Throwable failure) {
        for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
            if(e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && sql.getSQLState()!=null && sql.getSQLState().startsWith("08"))return false;
        for(Throwable e=failure;e!=null;e=e.getCause())
            if(e instanceof org.springframework.dao.QueryTimeoutException ||
               e instanceof java.sql.SQLTimeoutException ||
               e instanceof java.sql.SQLException sql && (sql.getErrorCode()==1205 || sql.getErrorCode()==1213))return true;
        return false;
    }
    private static final class SymbolLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
        SymbolLockFailure(Throwable cause) { super(cause); }
    }
    // A private marker cannot be supplied by a later business callback.
    private static final class InitialLockFailure extends RuntimeException {
        InitialLockFailure(RuntimeException cause) { super(cause); }
    }
    private static final class Expired extends RuntimeException {}
}

    private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
        return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
    }

    private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
                            String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
        PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
        if (paper == null) return;
        BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
        // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
        // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
        BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
                .add(remainingPnl, MC);
        paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
        paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
        paper.setStatus(status);
        paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
        paper.setExitReason(explanation);
        paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
        PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);

        // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
        // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
        // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
        productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
    }

        int effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
                Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
        BigDecimal positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);

                .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)

        // FIX-056: resolve a canonical execution-time price AFTER all signal/HTF gates but
        // BEFORE risk sizing and wallet persistence. Never size from a stale signal snapshot.
        ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice freshExecutionPrice = executionPriceAuthorityService
                .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
        if (freshExecutionPrice == null) {
            log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but no fresh canonical execution price was available; BUY skipped.",
                    executionSignal.getId());
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(
                executionSignal, executionDecision, freshExecutionPrice.price());
        if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
            log.info("BUY revalidation rejected signal {} at fresh price {}: {} - {}",
                    executionSignal.getId(), freshExecutionPrice.price(), executionDecision.code(), executionDecision.explanation());
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        // Recompute exposure after fresh-price Entry Quality because the final guard may
        // reduce the approved allocation further than the signal-time decision did.
        executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
        effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
                Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
        positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
        riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
                .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
                .multiply(positionScale, MC);
        BigDecimal riskPerUnit = freshExecutionPrice.price().subtract(executionSignal.getStopLoss(), MC).abs();

        if (riskPerUnit.signum() == 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid stop-loss distance");
        }

        BigDecimal riskModelQuantity = riskAmount.divide(riskPerUnit, MC);

        final boolean walletExecuted;
        try {
            ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
                    executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price());
            walletExecuted = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price(), freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(), effectivePositionPercent,
                    "Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "] " + executionDecision.explanation()
                            + " DecisionPrice=" + executionSignal.getLatestPrice()
                            + ", executionPrice=" + freshExecutionPrice.price()
                            + ", priceObservedAt=" + freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(),
                    executionDecision.source(),
                    entryQuality.score());
        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
            log.error("Automatic wallet BUY failed for signal {}: {}", executionSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        if (!walletExecuted) {
            log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
                .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
                .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
                .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
                .orElse(riskModelQuantity);

        executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
        PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
                .symbol(symbol)
                .side(PositionSide.BUY)
                .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
                .quantity(executedQuantity)
                .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
                .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
                .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
                .signal(executionSignal)
                .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
                .openedAt(Instant.now())
                .build());
        return Optional.of(position);
    }

    private PaperPosition completeClose(
            PaperPosition position,
            BigDecimal exitPrice,
            PositionStatus status,
            String closeReason,
            String explanation,
            TradeSignal exitSignal
    ) {
        BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
                .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
        // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
        BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
                .add(remainingPnl, MC);

        position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
        position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
        position.setStatus(status);
        position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
        position.setExitReason(explanation);
        position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
        position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
        PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);

        // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
        // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
        executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);

        if (exitSignal != null) {
            try {
                // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
                // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
                // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
                // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
                if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
                    walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
                } else {
                    walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
                }
            } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
                log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
            }
        }

        // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
        // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
        productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
        return saved;
    }

    private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
        }
        return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
    }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Scope remains exactly frozen `crypto_ai.paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitute trade was considered.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The permitted `db/CODEX/` evidence directory is empty. No executed result for DBQ-FTS-001..005, schema read, position row, wallet record, price event, audit record, or runtime log has been supplied to this track.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** No trade-specific cause is established. The six losses cannot yet be classified as valid stops, poor entries, delayed exits, protection defects, or accounting defects.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The prior source findings are only current-working-tree behavior, not proof of the historical deployed version/configuration for any frozen trade. This is material because entry, close, and audit paths have evolved.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current code has multiple terminal-ordering paths:
  - Signal-route closing writes `paper_position` before attempting the wallet exit, while catching wallet exceptions.
  - Live mechanical protection executes the wallet exit first and only then closes `paper_position`.
  
  Therefore a future paper/wallet mismatch is an investigation lead, not standalone proof of a failed or duplicated execution. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487) and [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Current `paper_position.realized_pnl` close arithmetic is price difference × quantity, preserving any prior partial-harvest P&L; it does not itself establish fee-adjusted net P&L. The frozen selection is valid as the supplied study predicate, but must not be described as a confirmed net-of-fee loss without wallet-fee evidence. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:487).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** An `APPLIED` `market_price_event` would establish a committed protection callback under current code, but not necessarily a position exit; the callback can be a no-op if no eligible open position exists. It must be joined to the affected position and wallet outcome. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:82).

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The snapshot labels differ by two seconds: UTC `2026-11-07 19:55:22` converts to KSA `2026-11-07 22:55:22`, while the supplied KSA label is `2026-11-07 22:55:20`. The frozen IDs remain authoritative; neither timestamp should be treated as a reconstructible exact intended-window clock.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I7-001  
Purpose: Establish actual schema before requesting joins; specifically determine which durable entry, wallet, protection, and exit-audit evidence is available for the frozen positions.  
SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'signal_processing_work',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'position_analysis',
      'execution_opportunity',
      'candle',
      'technical_indicator'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I7-002  
Purpose: Read only the frozen authoritative position rows’ supplied selection fields. This verifies current durable state only; it must not be treated as recreation of the missed snapshot.  
SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    p.id,
    p.status,
    p.closed_at,
    p.realized_pnl,
    CASE
        WHEN p.status = 'CLOSED'
         AND p.closed_at IS NOT NULL
         AND p.realized_pnl < 0
        THEN 1
        ELSE 0
    END AS currently_matches_study_predicate
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position AS p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);
```
### END_DB_REQUEST
